15:23:26.071 ==============================================================
15:23:26.071 BUS RELEASED
15:23:26.071 ==============================================================
15:23:26.071 ESP32 is no longer driving SDA/SCL. All TCA channels are closed
15:23:26.071 (clean baseline). BQ24195 is configured (IINLIM=2A, watchdog
15:23:26.071 disabled) and will retain that state indefinitely.
15:23:26.071 
15:23:26.071 Connect an Arduino Nano ESP32 (or any 3.3V Arduino) to:
15:23:26.071   Arduino SDA   <-> main PCB TPIO43 (SDA)
15:23:26.071   Arduino SCL   <-> main PCB TPIO44 (SCL)
15:23:26.072   Arduino GND   <-> main PCB GND test pad (shared ground required)
15:23:26.072 
15:23:26.072 Then flash and run:
15:23:26.072   tools/arduino-sketches/v7_bq_master_probe/v7_bq_master_probe.ino
15:23:26.072 
15:23:26.072 The Arduino sketch probes BQ on the closed-bus baseline, then opens
15:23:26.072 TCA channel 2 from its own master, and reports whether BQ ACKs in
15:23:26.072 each state. Expected outcomes:
15:23:26.072   - BQ NACKs Arduino on TCA2 -> Story D-revised confirmed
15:23:26.072   - BQ ACKs Arduino on TCA2  -> Story F confirmed
15:23:26.072 
15:23:26.072 ESP32 will print a heartbeat every 5 seconds so you can confirm it
15:23:26.574 is still alive while the Arduino has the bus. Press RESET to exit.

17:00:19.952 Connect an Arduino Nano ESP32 (or any 3.3V Arduino) to:
17:00:19.952   Arduino SDA   <-> main PCB TPIO43 (SDA)
17:00:19.952   Arduino SCL   <-> main PCB TPIO44 (SCL)
17:00:19.952   Arduino GND   <-> main PCB GND test pad (shared ground required)
17:00:19.952

17:00:19.952   tools/arduino-sketches/v7_bq_master_probe/v7_bq_master_probe.ino
17:00:19.952 
17:00:19.952 The Arduino sketch probes BQ on the closed-bus baseline, then opens
17:00:19.952 TCA channel 2 from its own master, and reports whether BQ ACKs in
17:00:19.952 each state. Expected outcomes:
17:00:19.952   - BQ NACKs Arduino on TCA2 -> Story D-revised confirmed
17:00:19.952   - BQ ACKs Arduino on TCA2  -> Story F confirmed

17:36:17.451 ==============================================================
17:36:17.451 BUS RELEASED
17:36:17.451 ==============================================================
17:36:17.451 ESP32 is no longer driving SDA/SCL. All TCA channels are closed
17:36:17.451 (clean baseline). BQ24195 is configured (IINLIM=2A, watchdog
17:36:17.451 disabled) and will retain that state indefinitely.
17:36:17.451 
17:36:17.451 Connect an Arduino Nano ESP32 (or any 3.3V Arduino) to:
17:36:17.451   Arduino SDA   <-> main PCB TPIO43 (SDA)
17:36:17.452   Arduino SCL   <-> main PCB TPIO44 (SCL)
17:36:17.452   Arduino GND   <-> main PCB GND test pad (shared ground required)
17:36:17.452 
17:36:17.452 Then flash and run:
17:36:17.452   tools/arduino-sketches/v7_bq_master_probe/v7_bq_master_probe.ino
17:36:17.452 
17:36:17.452 The Arduino sketch probes BQ on the closed-bus baseline, then opens
17:36:17.452 TCA channel 2 from its own master, and reports whether BQ ACKs in
17:36:17.452 each state. Expected outcomes:
17:36:17.452   - BQ NACKs Arduino on TCA2 -> Story D-revised confirmed
17:36:17.452   - BQ ACKs Arduino on TCA2  -> Story F confirmed
17:36:17.452 
17:36:17.956 ESP32 will print a heartbeat every 5 seconds so you can confirm it
17:36:17.956 is still alive while the Arduino has the bus. Press RESET to exit.

21:22:08.128 ==============================================================
21:22:08.128 ESP32 is no longer driving SDA/SCL. All TCA channels are closed
21:22:08.128 (clean baseline). BQ24195 is configured (IINLIM=2A, watchdog
21:22:08.129 disabled) and will retain that state indefinitely.
21:22:08.129 
21:22:08.129 Connect an Arduino Nano ESP32 (or any 3.3V Arduino) to:
21:22:08.129   Arduino SDA   <-> main PCB TPIO43 (SDA)
21:22:08.129   Arduino SCL   <-> main PCB TPIO44 (SCL)
21:22:08.129   Arduino GND   <-> main PCB GND test pad (shared ground required)
21:22:08.129 
21:22:08.129 Then flash and run:
21:22:08.129   tools/arduino-sketches/v7_bq_master_probe/v7_bq_master_probe.ino
21:22:08.129 
21:22:08.129 The Arduino sketch probes BQ on the closed-bus baseline, then opens
21:22:08.129 TCA channel 2 from its own master, and reports whether BQ ACKs in
21:22:08.129 each state. Expected outcomes:
21:22:08.129   - BQ NACKs Arduino on TCA2 -> Story D-revised confirmed
21:22:08.129   - BQ ACKs Arduino on TCA2  -> Story F confirmed
21:22:08.129 
21:22:08.129 ESP32 will print a heartbeat every 5 seconds so you can confirm it
21:22:08.631 is still alive while the Arduino has the bus. Press RESET to exit.
